# Clearance and callsign extraction: evaluation and testing

Deterministic parser/matcher, human-transcript upper bound, clearance-chain and oracle demonstrations; not an ASR model.

[Latest comparison](../evaluation.md) · [Every original source and cleanup status](source_inventory.json)

This is the single maintained history for this model/component. Records preserve the original bytes, including every numeric field, trial, seed, protocol, selection and caveat. Shared reports are included in each relevant ledger. No fresh inference is implied by consolidation.

## Recorded speech/extraction iterations

WER and accuracies are percentages. Different corpora and raw/numeral-normalized WER must not be ranked together. `NaN` means unavailable, never zero. Rejected number-rule results remain history only.
